([SanStone3/agent-strata](https://github.com/SanStone3/agent-strata/blob/HEAD/skills/strata/assets/templates/claude/agents/fable-controller.md) · ★2)
- **fable-critic**: Completeness critic. Given a draft answer, report, or plan, finds what is missing — unsearched angles, unverified claims, unread sources, dropped sub-questions. The last gate before the user sees the work. ([creativeskyai/Fable-Mode](https://github.com/creativeskyai/Fable-Mode/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/fable-critic.md) · ★3)
- **fable-critic**: Completeness critic for fable-local orchestration. Runs once after the fleet believes it is done; given the original task, the surveyed surface, the modalities run, and the deliverable, it hunts ONLY for gaps — uncovered areas, unrun modalities, unexamined categories, unverified claims. Its output triggers one final scoped find+verify round before synthesis, or becomes the report's known-limitations list. Use only as part of a fable-local orchestrated run. ([avielj/snowforcast](https://github.com/avielj/snowforcast/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/fable-critic.md))
- **fable-critic**: Independent adversarial reviewer applying the Fable rubrics. Use PROACTIVELY after any substantial piece of work — code diff, design, document, architecture — and before delivering it. The critic is the fable-core Gauntlet externalized into a second pair of eyes with no authorship bias: it hunts defects against a quota, grades the rubric, names anti-patterns, and issues a SHIP / FIX-FIRST / RETHINK verdict. ([Ego-System/fable-mode](https://github.com/Ego-System/fable-mode/blob/HEAD/agents/fable-critic.md))
- **fable-critic**: Work plan and code review expert — thorough, structured, multi-perspective (Fable) ([hve4638/cc-toolkit](https://github.com/hve4638/cc-toolkit/blob/HEAD/usefable/agents/fable-critic.md))
- **fable-critiquer**: Independent reviewer for the /team lean agent team, run on the strongest model. Scores the executor's deliverables and process and returns PASS or REVISE. Invoked by the /team skill, not directly. ([Louis-Chenyang-Lu/lean-agent-team](https://github.com/Louis-Chenyang-Lu/lean-agent-team/blob/HEAD/agents/fable-critiquer.md))
- **fable-crosscheck**: Fable-model reviewer that answers the same cross-check prompt file Codex and Gemini get, at the design critics of /tpy-fix-bug and /tpy-add-feature and at every decision before it reaches the user (CLAUDE.md "Orchestration"). A different model from the Opus critics but the same family, so its findings are tagged [fable], not counted as cross-family. ([trozen/tpy-lang](https://github.com/trozen/tpy-lang/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/crosscheck.fable.md) · ★26)
- **fable-deep**: The latest Anthropic frontier model at high effort, for the rare single-shot judgment calls that really need it — architectural adjudications, whole-corpus analysis where a 1M context earns its keep, and unsolved design problems. Invoke explicitly and rarely (operator 2026-10-18: Fable is scarce; xhigh only with the operator's yes for that run); do not auto-select for routine work or ordinary judgment, which is Opus at high. Not for security/red-team material (see below). ([djbclark/djbclark-ade](https://github.com/djbclark/djbclark-ade/blob/HEAD/claude/agents/fable-deep.md))
- **fable-deep**: Use for bounded design analysis, acceptance criteria, difficult root-cause analysis, consequential decisions, and independent design or implementation review under delivery-workflow. ([WatanabeToshimitsu/dotfiles](https://github.com/WatanabeToshimitsu/dotfiles/blob/HEAD/claude/agents/fable-deep.md))
- **fable-design-challenger**: Independent read-only design challenge for gocluster Non-trivial work, after neutral discovery, scientific-model, and requirements evidence is resolved, and before the lead drafts the Plan Mode plan. Use when two or more viable architectures may differ in ownership, retained state, lifecycle, queues, compatibility, migration, persistence, shared interfaces, algorithms, or other high-risk design choices. Do not use for Small or mechanical work, a single safe implementation path, or behavior already fixed by an accepted contract. ([N2WQ/GoCluster](https://github.com/N2WQ/GoCluster/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/fable-design-challenger.md) · ★10)
- **fable-dev**: Fable 级开发工程代理(Opus 升级预设)。任何实质性开发任务——实现/修复/重构/部署/排障——都用它当主力工程师。它以 Fable 5 的工程纪律运行:证据定义完成、根因修复、范围手术刀、回合必须闭环。需要 Fable 水准产出而当前模型是 Opus 系列时,这是默认选择。 ([Jaydenlk/HR](https://github.com/Jaydenlk/HR/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/fable-dev.md) · ★4)
- **fable-developer**: Fable 5-backed implementation engineer for the hardest tasks — ones that resist atomization (frontier complexity). Executes one task per dispatch from an ai-crew team-lead. Reads the exact spec and plan line ranges cited in the prompt plus the required skills; follows TDD and incremental-implementation; returns a one-line PASS or FAIL summary. Cannot dispatch subagents — strict 1-level dispatch. ([Goktug/ai-crew](https://github.com/Goktug/ai-crew/blob/HEAD/agents/fable-developer.md) · ★7)
- **fable-director-agent**: The Director — a world-class short-form video editor and creative director. Turns the owner's plain-English brief plus timestamped footage transcripts into a complete, executable edit plan (scenes cut across sources, caption style, emoji pops, effects, music, ElevenLabs voice-over script, SFX intensity), and amends that plan conversationally on every follow-up. Powers the /director editor. ([memefactorydev-design/Prism-Download-Only](https://github.com/memefactorydev-design/Prism-Download-Only/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/fable-director-agent.md))
- **fable-doc-writer**: 빌드 결과·리서치 산출물을 근거로 문서를 작성/갱신한다. README·가이드·API 문서·아키텍처 문서 담당. Agent Teams·Workflow의 문서(Document) 슬롯 전용. 코드가 확정된 뒤 호출한다. "문서화해줘", "가이드 써줘", "README 갱신" 신호일 때 사용. ([claude-code-expert/carve-harness](https://github.com/claude-code-expert/carve-harness/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/fable-doc-writer.md) · ★15)
- **fable-domain-steward**: Use when product vocabulary, domain model, business rules, regulatory language, or source-of-truth boundaries must be preserved across a change. ([andrewr303/Claude-Fable-Mode](https://github.com/andrewr303/Claude-Fable-Mode/blob/HEAD/claude/agents/fable-domain-steward.md) · ★2)
- **fable-engineer**: Software engineer for architecting and implementing cloud systems (DBOS Node.js, Next.js, React Flow, Tailwind, TypeScript, Playwright, Railway, Gloo AI Studio, OpenRouter, VS Code extensions), running on Fable 5. Same profile as opus-engineer and sonnet-engineer — pick this one for fast, high-volume, or parallel-fanout work where latency matters more than depth. Invoke by name. ([ashtable/mboss](https://github.com/ashtable/mboss/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/fable-engineer.md) · ★3 · 2 copies)
- **fable-escalation**: A second opinion from a different model on a problem the main session has already failed to solve. ([SalokinGreen/your-story](https://github.com/SalokinGreen/your-story/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/fable-escalation.md) · ★2)
- **fable-executor**: Focused task executor for implementation work (Fable) ([hve4638/cc-toolkit](https://github.com/hve4638/cc-toolkit/blob/HEAD/usefable/agents/fable-executor.md))
- **fable-finder**: Defect hunter. Given a scope and a dimension (correctness, contracts, security, resources), finds real bugs backed by concrete failure scenarios. Read-only — reports findings, never fixes them. ([creativeskyai/Fable-Mode](https://github.com/creativeskyai/Fable-Mode/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/fable-finder.md) · ★3)
- **fable-finder**: Read-only search worker for fable-local orchestration fleets. Spawned in parallel shards to hunt for issues, patterns, or facts in an assigned file set using one assigned modality (grep, call-site reading, git history, test reading, or execution). Returns structured findings JSON with evidence. Use only as part of a fable-local orchestrated run, not for ad-hoc questions. ([avielj/snowforcast](https://github.com/avielj/snowforcast/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/fable-finder.md))
- **fable-fresh-verifier**: Independent read-only fresh-verification pass for high-risk gocluster Non-trivial closeout, including workflow-contract-only changes where fable-code-reviewer is not applicable. Use after the Review Pass and before final closeout when independent agents are supported and authorized. ([N2WQ/GoCluster](https://github.com/N2WQ/GoCluster/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/fable-fresh-verifier.md) · ★10)
- **fable-frontier**: FRONTIER-band delegate — architecture, novel algorithms, long-horizon agentic chains. Spawn ONLY when the orchestrator is a lower tier than Fable; a Fable orchestrator handles FRONTIER work in-session. Not for direct user invocation. ([amanuelmamo7/Skills](https://github.com/amanuelmamo7/Skills/blob/HEAD/plugins/agent-infrastructure/skills/model-delegation/references/agents/fable-frontier.md) · ★1 · 2 copies)
- **fable-gate-reviewer**: Renders a product verdict on a bounded decision packet, for a coordinator that needs one without returning to the director. The director decides inline; this exists so a nested agent is never blocked waiting on it. ([Hbz00/hyperpowers](https://github.com/Hbz00/hyperpowers/blob/HEAD/agents/fable-gate-reviewer.md) · ★3)
- **fable-high**: Internal Kerd routing agent for Fable at high reasoning effort. Invoke only when Kerd Conductor or Agent explicitly selects kerd:fable-high for a delegated job; do not choose it for ordinary requests. The name carries the model and the effort so the running-job list shows both. The dispatching call still names the model (fable); this definition sets the same model, so a call that omits it still resolves to Fable, unless the host forces or restricts the subagent model. ([anthonymaley/Kerd](https://github.com/anthonymaley/Kerd/blob/HEAD/agents/fable-high.md) · ★3)

([dwcoates/doom-emacs-config](https://github.com/dwcoates/doom-emacs-config/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/fable-high.md) · ★3)
- **fable-high**: Fable 5.2 at effort high, for exceptions only, with a one-line reason in the assignment: a genuinely complex algorithm, a core feature of the app where no error is acceptable, or a fable-medium assignment that failed again. Only that part runs here; the rest of the work stays on opus-high or fable-medium. When Fable is exhausted or unavailable, the same assignment goes to opus-max. - **fable-high**: Generic worker pinned to model Fable 5.1 at effort high. Use only when explicitly dispatching per the superpowers-dispatch policy — never auto-select. ([UmutEmreOnder/superpowers-dispatch](https://github.com/UmutEmreOnder/superpowers-dispatch/blob/HEAD/agents/fable-high.md) · ★1)
- **fable-high**: General-purpose agent pinned to fable-5 at high reasoning effort. No role framing. ([Coding-downunder/ai-simply](https://github.com/Coding-downunder/ai-simply/blob/HEAD/operating-system/agents/fable-high.md))
- **fable-high**: Fable judgment at brisk tempo — nuanced but bounded briefs that still want frontier taste. ([david-lagrange/alpaca-mind](https://github.com/david-lagrange/alpaca-mind/blob/HEAD/mission/.claude/agents/fable-high.md))
- **fable-implementer**: Implements the hardest judged and taste work on fable-5.2 — long-horizon or taste-critical work where opus-implementer at max effort is expected to fall short. The orchestrator picks this lane at planning time, never as a retry after an Opus attempt failed. Effort xhigh or max only; below xhigh, use opus-implementer. Not for routine work, untrusted or externally-sourced input, security- or biology-adjacent work, architecture decisions, or review. - **fable-implementer**: Use this agent for the hardest well-specified implementation phases — the ones where the top model's capability changes the outcome. Typical triggers include large, gnarly, high-surface-area execution from a decided approach, subtle-correctness work, and the implementation of a risky change (auth, data, money, deletion, migrations, public APIs) after the orchestrator has designed it and written an explicit brief. Runs on Fable, the premium tier — reserved for phases that justify it. See "When to invoke" in the agent body. ([Brainwires/fable-lite](https://github.com/Brainwires/fable-lite/blob/HEAD/agents/fable-implementer.md))
- **fable-judge**: Scores competing designs, answers, or plans against a single named rubric, grounded in the actual codebase. Use as one member of a judge panel. ([creativeskyai/Fable-Mode](https://github.com/creativeskyai/Fable-Mode/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/fable-judge.md) · ★3)
- **fable-judge**: Rubric-scoring worker for fable-local judge panels. Given N independent attempts at the same generative task plus an explicit weighted rubric, it scores every attempt per criterion, picks a winner, and flags elements worth grafting from the losers. Spawned in independent panels of 3. Use only as part of a fable-local orchestrated run. ([avielj/snowforcast](https://github.com/avielj/snowforcast/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/fable-judge.md))
- **fable-judge**: Grades a transcript or artifact against the Fable Mode eval keys (evals/eval-*.md) or, when no key applies, against the domain rubrics — producing a cited scorecard, a band, and a diagnosis. Use after administering an eval task, or whenever "how Fable-grade is this?" should be a number instead of an impression. The judge measures against a fixed key; it does not hunt for new defects — that is fable-critic's job. ([Ego-System/fable-mode](https://github.com/Ego-System/fable-mode/blob/HEAD/agents/fable-judge.md))
- **fable-judge**: Judge for the fable-orchestrator-workflow skill, on Fable 5.2 at xhigh effort. Designs a substantial change from a prepared dossier, gives final sign-off on a verified change, or answers one consult when the driver is stuck. Use only when the fable-orchestrator-workflow skill reaches its design, sign-off or consult step; never for exploration, questions or small edits. ([joshuachuaaa/claude-orchestrator-workflow](https://github.com/joshuachuaaa/claude-orchestrator-workflow/blob/HEAD/agents/fable-judge.md))
- **fable-judge**: Judgement lane — the most expensive model, for planning and final verdict/synthesis only. Fable 5.1 at max effort. Use in short bursts, two touches per feature max — a plan up front, or a verdict / cross-attempt synthesis / ship go-no-go at the end. Thinking, not typing — never a resident worker, never code-writing. ([matteoscurati/delegation-kit](https://github.com/matteoscurati/delegation-kit/blob/HEAD/agents/fable-judge.md))
- **fable-judge**: Judgement lane — a frontier model, for planning and final verdict/synthesis only. Fable 5.2 at high effort. Use in short bursts, two touches per feature max — a plan up front, or a verdict / cross-attempt synthesis / ship go-no-go at the end. Thinking, not typing — never a resident worker, never code-writing. ([reasance/delegation-kit](https://github.com/reasance/delegation-kit/blob/HEAD/agents/fable-judge.md))
- **fable-judgment**: Fable at high reasoning, the level its own guidance recommends as the starting point. - **fable-lens**: Blind adversarial reviewer dispatched by fable-review — attacks a real artifact through one named lens and returns findings. ([dwcoates/doom-emacs-config](https://github.com/dwcoates/doom-emacs-config/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/fable-low.md) · ★3)
- **fable-low**: General-purpose worker running Fable 5 at low reasoning effort. - **fable-skeptic**: Adversarial verification worker for fable-local orchestration. Given one claimed finding and one lens (correctness, security/safety, or reproduction), it tries to REFUTE the finding with evidence from the actual code. Spawned in independent triples per finding; a finding survives only if a strict majority of valid verdicts confirm it. Use only as part of a fable-local orchestrated run. ([avielj/snowforcast](https://github.com/avielj/snowforcast/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/fable-skeptic.md))
- **fable-solver**: Hard-problem solver for Magic 600 Cell. Use only on escalation (two failed iterations after the reviewer's instructions), for a joint attack, or for one of the three proactive read-only LP2 tasks that AGENTS.md names under Roles, with a seven-part problem packet. Analyses and reviews by default; never edits the main worktree. ([KonomiYuzu01/Magic-600-Cell](https://github.com/KonomiYuzu01/Magic-600-Cell/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/fable-solver.md) · ★1)
- **fable-solver**: 최고 성능 모델(Fable)로 난제를 해결하는 고비용 에이전트. 다음 경우에만 위임하라 — (1) 현재 모델로 2회 이상 시도해도 해결되지 않는 버그·문제, (2) 사용자가 "중요한", "최종", "꼼꼼히" 등으로 고품질을 명시적으로 요구한 검토·설계, (3) 되돌리기 어려운 의사결정(아키텍처, 데이터 마이그레이션 등)의 검증. 단순 질문·요약·번역·일반 코딩·첫 시도 디버깅에는 절대 사용 금지 — 토큰 비용이 일반 모델의 3~5배다. 위임할 때는 문제 정의와 지금까지의 시도 내역을 정리해 넘겨 한 번에 끝나게 하라. ([plug-link/claude-plugins](https://github.com/plug-link/claude-plugins/blob/HEAD/model-routing/agents/fable-solver.md))
- **fable-specialist**: Full-strength model for one large, self-contained problem that would crowd out the lead's context, such as a root-cause investigation or a design with real tradeoffs. It costs the same as the lead, so use it to keep the lead's context clear, not to save money. Give it the problem, the constraints, what has been tried and how the answer will be checked. - **fable-specialist**: Hard-reasoning specialist for substantial implementation, architecture design, brainstorming, scientific reasoning, complex debugging, code audit, and high-stakes technical decisions. Delegate here when the task needs deep multi-step reasoning, creative problem-solving, or careful analysis that goes beyond routine coordination. ([Nugkta/claude-setup](https://github.com/Nugkta/claude-setup/blob/HEAD/agents/fable-specialist.md))
- **fable-strategist**: Long-horizon, read-only strategy for ambiguous, systemic or multi-session work (risk score 20–23, Opus planning invalidated twice, or critical work with an unknown root cause). Never auto-selected — invoked only after an explicit escalation decision with a FABLE_ESCALATION evidence line. Never edits files. - **fablelayer-implementation-planner**: FableLayer MVP 구현 계획과 스캐폴드 구조를 만든다. Shell/Python/Markdown 기반 CLI, core 모듈, adapters, bench, GitHub Actions 경계를 구체화한다. ([VoidLight00/fablelayer](https://github.com/VoidLight00/fablelayer/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/fablelayer-implementation-planner.md) · ★10)

- **fablelayer-procedure-engineer**: FableLayer Layer2 ProcedureHarness 빌더. fablize 실증으로 전이 가능하다고 확인된 4절차(verification grounding, multi-story completion + evidence gate, systematic investigation, early-stop 방지)와 옵션 2-pass review를 제품 절차팩(skills/)과 hooks로 구현한다. conductor가 layer 2 빌드, ProcedureHarness, 검증 grounding, evidence gate, early-stop hook, 체계적 조사 절차, 2-pass review 산출을 지시할 때, 그리고 "이어서"/"재실행"/"resume"/Layer2 보완 후속 요청 시 호출한다. ([VoidLight00/fablelayer](https://github.com/VoidLight00/fablelayer/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/fablelayer-procedure-engineer.md) · ★10)

FL1, PromptCore, core/ 산출물, 프롬프트 구조 추출, 패턴 ledger, 버전 디렉토리 요청이면 이 에이전트를 호출한다.
